Make competing outcome claims visible before choosing measures or redesigning tasks; a purchaser’s preference is not automatically every affected person’s interest.</p></dd><dt>Communication</dt><dd><p>Recipients, performers and sponsors compare what they regard as successful completion and record unresolved differences.</p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Responsible managers can commit resources; beneficiaries and affected workers contribute outcome evidence. ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S002</p></li><li><p>S011</p></li><li><p>S014</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. No historical founder is asserted for analytical composition or stress propositions.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_ANALYTICAL_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Original form</dt><dd><p>A process contains activities with identifiable outcomes and a case or instance perspective.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>Identify a defensible unit of end-to-end work and its purpose before intervening.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>Artificial closure hides unresolved obligations; one event is attributed to the wrong case; open-ended work is distorted.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>State what initiates the work, what object or case is being advanced and what counts as completion, cancellation or continuing responsibility. Do not force a single stable case identifier or a terminal event onto open-ended knowledge work without justification.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>Measurement, discovery or enactment depends on assigning work to cases and deciding whether work is finished.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>Use a lightweight episode or work-object account; where no stable repeated structure exists, coordinate goals and obligations without heavy process standardisation.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p>A defensible unit of work; treatment of shared/multiple objects; <a id="EBPM-EBPM-006"></a>
## EBPM:EBPM-006

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-001.md#EBPM-EBPM-006">EBPM:EBPM-006</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-006</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Triangulated discovery of performed work</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B2</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S002</p></li><li><p>S025</p></li><li><p>S048</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Stress propositions are not attributed to the whole field; composition properties are this researcher’s synthesis.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_ANALYTICAL_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/19.md#r-19063a7255fb442b64d0b6b1ccac369c4ef07919734c2d04b1a5ba375d0b05cc">Complete shared evidence limit · 19063a7255fb</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Accounts of performed work, variants and observations outside current models</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>The as-is account identifies its sources, covered variants and consequential uncertainties.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Compare interviews, documents, walkthroughs, direct observation and event evidence where they reveal different aspects of work. Record provenance and disagreement; neither a remembered account, a prescription nor an event log is intrinsically the whole truth.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A decision depends on what actually happens and available accounts may be incomplete, selective or incentive-distorted.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>For a transparent low-risk process, a few representative walkthroughs with performers and recipients may suffice; all evidence channels are not compulsory.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Access to relevant participants and cases; a sampling rationale; permission to observe work without disproportionate intrusion.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/70.md#r-70746141da1d9982bc61f43441fc7dc53079df89d77d53a73c04d80e345eb0a3">Complete shared assumptions · 70746141da1d</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analysts synthesise evidence; performers and recipients can correct it. Sponsorship does not entitle an analyst to dismiss inconvenient observations.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Bring discrepant accounts back to the people involved and distinguish uncertainty from disagreement about the desired process.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/83.md#r-83d058468323dc6790825981217427b5063d74c59c4f39434d6f07cd78651a74">Complete shared consumer · 83d058468323</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The as-is account identifies its sources, covered variants and consequential uncertainties.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Observation changes behaviour; workshop consensus conceals dissent; system logs omit essential coordination.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Observation changes behaviour; workshop consensus conceals dissent; system logs omit essential coordination.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S041’s account of work outside workflow categories and S035’s data-quality principles defeat unqualified ground-truth claims. Triangulation can still share the same bias.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S002">EBPM:S002</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S025">EBPM:S025</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-004.md#EBPM-S035">EBPM:S035</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S041">EBPM:S041</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S048">EBPM:S048</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R002</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R003</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R025</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R026</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R029</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R034</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R036</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R044</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R047</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R051</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>For a transparent low-risk process, a few representative walkthroughs with performers and recipients may suffice; all evidence channels are not compulsory.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Which independent channel is likely to expose a decision-relevant omission?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/b3.md#r-b3c5c3d73f97387122ddda3c3b766985669f9c10b8119d61e4cb0bb11f5a3e51">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · b3c5c3d73f97</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Retained as a robust criterion/mechanism distinction within the declared trigger; this is not a claim of universally quantified economic benefit. Discovery is refined from producing one approved map to establishing a bounded, source-aware account of performed work.</p></dd><dt>Original namespace Preserve disagreement about facts, goals and authority separately; endorsement of a picture is not evidence of shared decision meaning.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A process model or redesign will be used across roles with different knowledge or incentives.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A brief case walkthrough or annotated narrative may establish the needed understanding without a workshop series.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Relevant voices can participate safely; the model’s purpose is explicit; disagreements have a decision route.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/c2.md#r-c2c025e2973a592a2535ac17b5bdc9493524670757f06a6e82a8c5fc965b8d74">Complete shared assumptions · c2c025e2973a</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Participants validate their knowledge, not necessarily the whole process; sponsors resolve authorised choices without erasing dissent.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Ask participants to explain what happens in discriminating cases and identify omissions or conflicting interpretations.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/87.md#r-873230a860ced664933011b3850142fe1637c69ff04fa57902882aa00821fcdc">Complete shared consumer · 873230a860ce</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>Users can apply the representation to the relevant case distinction, or a specific unresolved disagreement is recorded.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Power imbalances manufacture agreement; frontline time is consumed without influence; validation becomes sign-off theatre.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Power imbalances manufacture agreement; frontline time is consumed without influence; validation becomes sign-off theatre.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S030 raises affected-person and fairness issues; S041 shows one representation can serve different organisational purposes. Consensus is not a universal correctness test.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S005">EBPM:S005</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S025">EBPM:S025</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S030">EBPM:S030</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S041">EBPM:S041</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S048">EBPM:S048</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R003</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R004</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R025</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A brief case walkthrough or annotated narrative may establish the needed understanding without a workshop series.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><ol><li><p>How can marginal participants contest a representation without retaliation or excessive cost?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/5c.md#r-5c62ac3ee27b4183d3f134d450c115f462016bafeafcc846ad95df7674b3717f">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · 5c62ac3ee27b</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Retained only in the stated criticism-tested form, with authority, evidence and consequence limits preserved.
